People are starting to notice that I'm just slower than I used to be.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling stressed or overwhelmed at work or in your personal life lately?\n\nPatient: I don't know. Work is just work. I guess it's fine. I'm just... tired, maybe.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel tired all the time?\n\nPatient: Yeah, pretty much. Just... always tired. Even when I sleep. It doesn't really go away.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down lately?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I guess so. It's just... most days I wake up and I just have this... this heavy, flat sadness in my chest. It kind of makes me feel like... like there's no point in trying to build the life I was planning. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Do you find pleasure in activities you used to enjoy, like styling hair or anything else outside of work?\n\nPatient: Not really. I used to... I used to like talking to my clients, you know? And helping the new girls at the salon. Sometimes I'd go hiking with my friends on the weekends. But now... I just kind of keep my head down. I try to avoid people. I just... don't really have that spark anymore.\n\nDoctor: Has something very upsetting or traumatic happened to you recently?\n\nPatient: No. Not really. Just... normal stuff, I guess. Nothing big.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty about anything?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess. I just... I don't know. I feel like I'm just dragging everyone down. Not really on edge or anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you still enjoy eating your favorite foods?\n\nPatient: Um... not really. I just... haven't really been thinking about food. But... yeah, I just don't really have an appetite.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really hurting myself. I don't... I don't have a plan or anything. Just... sometimes I think... I don't know. Like maybe it would just be easier if I didn't wake up. Then... no one would have to deal with me anymore. But... no, I wouldn't actually do anything.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this heavy, flat sadness?\n\nPatient: Um... a while, I guess. I don't really know exactly when it started. But... maybe like a year? Or... a year and two months, probably. I guess it's most days. Like... looking back at everything, I've spent most of this whole time just... feeling weighed down. There are some shorter stretches where it's not as bad, I guess. But... mostly it's just heavy.\n\nDoctor: Would you say your heavy, flat sadness is mild, moderate, or severe most of the time?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know. Moderate, I guess. It's just... always kind of there.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in or enjoy doing?\n\nPatient: Um... not really. I used to... I don't know. It's just... the same, mostly. I just try to get through the day.\n\nDoctor: Have you had at least two months where your symptoms were much better at any point since they started?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I don't think so. It's just... been heavy for a while now. I haven't really had a break like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that your sadness or energy levels change at all with the seasons?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know. I haven't really paid attention to that. It just... always feels the same.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
